⏺ The project compiles successfully. Here's a summary of what I created:

  Project Structure

  Documentation (docs/):
  - prd.md - Product requirements document
  - architecture.md - System architecture with diagrams
  - design.md - UI/UX design specs and theme colors
  - plan.md - Implementation plan with phases
  - status.md - Current project status (complete)

  Source Code (src/):
  - main.rs - Entry point, initializes Yew renderer
  - app.rs - Root component with theme state and switching logic
  - lorem.rs - Lorem ipsum text constants
  - components/ - All UI components:
    - header.rs - Site header with branding and theme selector
    - theme_selector.rs - Dropdown for theme switching
    - nav.rs - Left sidebar navigation
    - main_content.rs - Main content with sections and articles
    - aside.rs - Right sidebar with links, info, tags
    - footer.rs - Site footer

  Styles (styles/):
  - simple-light.css - Light theme (white background, dark text)
  - simple-dark.css - Dark theme (dark background, light text)

  Both themes use CSS custom properties and CSS Grid for the layout.

  Entry Point:
  - index.html - Trunk-compatible HTML with theme stylesheet link

  To Run

  # Install trunk if needed
  cargo install trunk

  # Start dev server
  trunk serve

  The app demonstrates all HTML5 semantic layout elements (<header>, <nav>, <main>, <section>, <article>, <aside>, <footer>) with a
  dropdown in the header to switch between light and dark themes dynamically—all in pure Rust/WASM with no JavaScript.

       11 +  #[function_component(App)]
       12 +  fn app() -> Html {
       13 +      let theme = use_state(|| "simple-light".to_string());
       14 +
       15 +      let on_theme_change = {
       16 +          let theme = theme.clone();
       17 +          Callback::from(move |new_theme: String| {
       18 +              set_theme(&new_theme);
       19 +              theme.set(new_theme);
       20 +          })
       21 +      };
       22 +
       23 +      html! {
       24 +          <div class="page-layout">
       25 +              <Header theme={(*theme).clone()} on_theme_change={on_theme_change} />
       26 +              <Nav />
       27 +              <MainContent />
       28 +              <Aside />
       29 +              <Footer />
       30 +          </div>
       31 +      }
       32 +  }
       33 +
       34 +  fn set_theme(theme: &str) {
       35 +      let window = web_sys::window().expect("no window");
       36 +      let document = window.document().expect("no document");
       37 +
       38 +      if let Some(link) = document.get_element_by_id("theme-stylesheet") {
       39 +          if let Ok(link) = link.dyn_into::<web_sys::HtmlLinkElement>() {
       40 +              link.set_href(&format!("styles/{theme}.css"));
       41 +          }
       42 +      }
       43 +  }
       44 +
       45    fn main() {
       46        wasm_logger::init(wasm_logger::Config::default());
       47        yew::Renderer::<App>::new().render();

⏺ The skill is set up. Here's a summary of what was created:

  Claude UI Styling Skill Created

  Location

  .claude/skills/ui-styling/
  +-- SKILL.md      # Main skill definition with YAML frontmatter
  +-- patterns.md   # Reference file with CSS patterns and palettes

  How It Works

  1. Automatic Discovery: Claude Code automatically discovers skills in .claude/skills/ directories
  2. Model-Invoked: Unlike slash commands (which you type explicitly), skills are activated by Claude when your request matches the
  skill's description
  3. Description Triggers: The skill description includes trigger terms like:
    - "create new themes"
    - "improve CSS styling"
    - "add visual polish"
    - "make UIs more modern/attractive"
    - "implement design systems"

  How to Use It

  Simply ask Claude to do UI/styling work naturally:

  - "Create a modern minimalist theme"
  - "Add a vibrant, colorful theme to the project"
  - "Make the UI more modern and polished"
  - "Create a dark theme with a purple color scheme"
  - "Improve the CSS styling with better shadows and transitions"

  Claude will automatically use the skill's guidance, including:
  - The project's CSS custom properties convention
  - Color palette suggestions from patterns.md
  - Animation and shadow patterns
  - Accessibility considerations

  To Verify the Skill

  You can ask: "What skills are available?" and Claude should list the ui-styling skill.

  To Add More Themes

  After creating new CSS files with the skill's help, you'll also need to update src/components/header.rs to add the new theme
  option to the dropdown selector.

  Would you like me to commit this skill to the repository, or would you like to test it first by asking me to create a new theme?

│ .claude/skills/wild-ui-styling/patterns.md                                                                                           │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ # Wild CSS Patterns Reference                                                                                                        │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ## Gradient Background Patterns                                                                                                      │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Animated Gradient                                                                                                                │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes gradientShift {                                                                                                           │
│     0% { background-position: 0% 50%; }                                                                                              │
│     50% { background-position: 100% 50%; }                                                                                           │
│     100% { background-position: 0% 50%; }                                                                                            │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │
│     background: linear-gradient(-45deg, #ee7752, #e73c7e, #23a6d5, #23d5ab);                                                         │
│     background-size: 400% 400%;                                                                                                      │
│     animation: gradientShift 15s ease infinite;                                                                                      │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Mesh Gradient                                                                                                                    │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │
│     background-color: #ff99ee;                                                                                                       │
│     background-image:                                                                                                                │
│         radial-gradient(at 40% 20%, #ff99ee 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                   │
│         radial-gradient(at 80% 0%, #ffd700 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                    │
│         radial-gradient(at 0% 50%, #00ffff 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                    │
│         radial-gradient(at 80% 50%, #ff6b6b 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                   │
│         radial-gradient(at 0% 100%, #7b68ee 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                   │
│         radial-gradient(at 80% 100%, #00fa9a 0px, transparent 50%);                                                                  │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Diagonal Stripes                                                                                                                 │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │
│     background: repeating-linear-gradient(                                                                                           │
│         45deg,                                                                                                                       │
│         #606dbc,                                                                                                                     │
│         #606dbc 10px,                                                                                                                │
│         #465298 10px,                                                                                                                │
│         #465298 20px                                                                                                                 │
│     );                                                                                                                               │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Conic Gradient Burst                                                                                                             │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │
│     background: conic-gradient(                                                                                                      │
│         from 0deg at 50% 50%,                                                                                                        │
│         #ff0080, #ff8c00, #40e0d0,                                                                                                   │
│         #ff0080, #ff8c00, #40e0d0,                                                                                                   │
│         #ff0080                                                                                                                      │
│     );                                                                                                                               │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ## Text Rotation Effects                                                                                                             │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Tilted Heading                                                                                                                   │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ h1 {                                                                                                                                 │
│     transform: rotate(-3deg);                                                                                                        │
│     transform-origin: left center;                                                                                                   │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Dramatic Tilt with Shadow                                                                                                        │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ .wild-heading {                                                                                                                      │
│     transform: rotate(-5deg) skewX(-5deg);                                                                                           │
│     text-shadow:                                                                                                                     │
│         3px 3px 0 #ff00ff,                                                                                                           │
│         6px 6px 0 #00ffff;                                                                                                           │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Perspective Text                                                                                                                 │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ .perspective-text {                                                                                                                  │
│     transform: perspective(500px) rotateX(15deg);                                                                                    │
│     transform-origin: bottom center;                                                                                                 │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Hover Rotation                                                                                                                   │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ h2 {                                                                                                                                 │
│     transition: transform 0.3s ease;                                                                                                 │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ h2:hover {                                                                                                                           │
│     transform: rotate(2deg) scale(1.05);                                                                                             │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ## Fade-In Animations                                                                                                                │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Simple Fade In                                                                                                                   │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes fadeIn {                                                                                                                  │
│     from { opacity: 0; }                                                                                                             │
│     to { opacity: 1; }                                                                                                               │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ .fade-in {                                                                                                                           │
│     animation: fadeIn 0.8s ease-out forwards;                                                                                        │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Fade In Up                                                                                                                       │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes fadeInUp {                                                                                                                │
│     from {                                                                                                                           │
│         opacity: 0;                                                                                                                  │
│         transform: translateY(30px);                                                                                                 │
│     }                                                                                                                                │
│     to {                                                                                                                             │
│         opacity: 1;                                                                                                                  │
│         transform: translateY(0);                                                                                                    │
│     }                                                                                                                                │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ .fade-in-up {                                                                                                                        │
│     animation: fadeInUp 0.6s ease-out forwards;                                                                                      │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Fade In Scale                                                                                                                    │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes fadeInScale {                                                                                                             │
│     from {                                                                                                                           │
│         opacity: 0;                                                                                                                  │
│         transform: scale(0.9);                                                                                                       │
│     }                                                                                                                                │
│     to {                                                                                                                             │

│ }                                                                                                                                    │

│ .stagger-item:nth-child(3) { animation-delay: 0.3s; }                                                                                │
│ .stagger-item:nth-child(4) { animation-delay: 0.4s; }                                                                                │

> I want to generate a Yew/Rust/WASM app that demonstrates an HTML5 layout using sidebars, sections, etc. (the layout-specific HTML5
 elements) with filler content (lorem ipsum), to show different CSS stylings.  There should be a "theme" drop-down that selects
different css files dynamically to change the style.  Start with two themes simple-dark and simple-light that use CSS to support a
dark or light mode.  Start with a new docs dir and new markdown files: architecture, prd, design, plan, and status.  Use Rust for
all domain and presentation logic (minimal JavaScript if any).  Start with vanilla CSS.

⏺ I'll create a Yew/Rust/WASM app that demonstrates HTML5 layout elements with theme switching. Let me start by setting up the
  documentation and then the implementation.

⏺ The project compiles successfully. Here's a summary of what I created:

  Project Structure

  Documentation (docs/):
  - prd.md - Product requirements document
  - architecture.md - System architecture with diagrams
  - design.md - UI/UX design specs and theme colors
  - plan.md - Implementation plan with phases
  - status.md - Current project status (complete)

  Source Code (src/):
  - main.rs - Entry point, initializes Yew renderer
  - app.rs - Root component with theme state and switching logic
  - lorem.rs - Lorem ipsum text constants
  - components/ - All UI components:
    - header.rs - Site header with branding and theme selector
    - theme_selector.rs - Dropdown for theme switching
    - nav.rs - Left sidebar navigation
    - main_content.rs - Main content with sections and articles
    - aside.rs - Right sidebar with links, info, tags
    - footer.rs - Site footer

  Styles (styles/):
  - simple-light.css - Light theme (white background, dark text)
  - simple-dark.css - Dark theme (dark background, light text)

  Both themes use CSS custom properties and CSS Grid for the layout.

  Entry Point:
  - index.html - Trunk-compatible HTML with theme stylesheet link

  To Run

  # Install trunk if needed
  cargo install trunk

  # Start dev server
  trunk serve

  The app demonstrates all HTML5 semantic layout elements (<header>, <nav>, <main>, <section>, <article>, <aside>, <footer>) with a
  dropdown in the header to switch between light and dark themes dynamically—all in pure Rust/WASM with no JavaScript.

        1 -  mod app;
        1 +  //! Styles POC - A Yew/WASM demo of HTML5 semantic layouts with theme switching.
        2 +
        3    mod components;
        4    mod lorem;
        5
        6 -  use app::App;
        6 +  use wasm_bindgen::JsCast;

        9 +  use components::{footer::Footer, header::Header, main_content::MainContent, sidebars::{Aside, Nav}};
       10 +
       11 +  #[function_component(App)]
       12 +  fn app() -> Html {
       13 +      let theme = use_state(|| "simple-light".to_string());
       14 +
       15 +      let on_theme_change = {
       16 +          let theme = theme.clone();
       17 +          Callback::from(move |new_theme: String| {
       18 +              set_theme(&new_theme);
       19 +              theme.set(new_theme);
       20 +          })
       21 +      };
       22 +
       23 +      html! {
       24 +          <div class="page-layout">
       25 +              <Header theme={(*theme).clone()} on_theme_change={on_theme_change} />
       26 +              <Nav />
       27 +              <MainContent />
       28 +              <Aside />
       29 +              <Footer />
       30 +          </div>
       31 +      }
       32 +  }
       33 +
       34 +  fn set_theme(theme: &str) {
       35 +      let window = web_sys::window().expect("no window");
       36 +      let document = window.document().expect("no document");
       37 +
       38 +      if let Some(link) = document.get_element_by_id("theme-stylesheet") {
       39 +          if let Ok(link) = link.dyn_into::<web_sys::HtmlLinkElement>() {
       40 +              link.set_href(&format!("styles/{theme}.css"));
       41 +          }
       42 +      }
       43 +  }
       44 +
       45    fn main() {
       46        wasm_logger::init(wasm_logger::Config::default());
       47        yew::Renderer::<App>::new().render();

⏺ The skill is set up. Here's a summary of what was created:

  Claude UI Styling Skill Created

  Location

  .claude/skills/ui-styling/
  +-- SKILL.md      # Main skill definition with YAML frontmatter
  +-- patterns.md   # Reference file with CSS patterns and palettes

  How It Works

  1. Automatic Discovery: Claude Code automatically discovers skills in .claude/skills/ directories
  2. Model-Invoked: Unlike slash commands (which you type explicitly), skills are activated by Claude when your request matches the
  skill's description
  3. Description Triggers: The skill description includes trigger terms like:
    - "create new themes"
    - "improve CSS styling"
    - "add visual polish"
    - "make UIs more modern/attractive"
    - "implement design systems"

  How to Use It

  Simply ask Claude to do UI/styling work naturally:

  - "Create a modern minimalist theme"
  - "Add a vibrant, colorful theme to the project"
  - "Make the UI more modern and polished"
  - "Create a dark theme with a purple color scheme"
  - "Improve the CSS styling with better shadows and transitions"

  Claude will automatically use the skill's guidance, including:
  - The project's CSS custom properties convention
  - Color palette suggestions from patterns.md
  - Animation and shadow patterns
  - Accessibility considerations

  To Verify the Skill

  You can ask: "What skills are available?" and Claude should list the ui-styling skill.

  To Add More Themes

  After creating new CSS files with the skill's help, you'll also need to update src/components/header.rs to add the new theme
  option to the dropdown selector.

  Would you like me to commit this skill to the repository, or would you like to test it first by asking me to create a new theme?

│ # Wild CSS Patterns Reference                                                                                                        │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ## Gradient Background Patterns                                                                                                      │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Animated Gradient                                                                                                                │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes gradientShift {                                                                                                           │
│     0% { background-position: 0% 50%; }                                                                                              │
│     50% { background-position: 100% 50%; }                                                                                           │
│     100% { background-position: 0% 50%; }                                                                                            │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │
│     background: linear-gradient(-45deg, #ee7752, #e73c7e, #23a6d5, #23d5ab);                                                         │
│     background-size: 400% 400%;                                                                                                      │
│     animation: gradientShift 15s ease infinite;                                                                                      │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Mesh Gradient                                                                                                                    │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │
│     background-color: #ff99ee;                                                                                                       │
│     background-image:                                                                                                                │
│         radial-gradient(at 40% 20%, #ff99ee 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                   │
│         radial-gradient(at 80% 0%, #ffd700 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                    │
│         radial-gradient(at 0% 50%, #00ffff 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                    │
│         radial-gradient(at 80% 50%, #ff6b6b 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                   │
│         radial-gradient(at 0% 100%, #7b68ee 0px, transparent 50%),                                                                   │
│         radial-gradient(at 80% 100%, #00fa9a 0px, transparent 50%);                                                                  │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Diagonal Stripes                                                                                                                 │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │
│     background: repeating-linear-gradient(                                                                                           │
│         45deg,                                                                                                                       │
│         #606dbc,                                                                                                                     │
│         #606dbc 10px,                                                                                                                │
│         #465298 10px,                                                                                                                │
│         #465298 20px                                                                                                                 │
│     );                                                                                                                               │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Conic Gradient Burst                                                                                                             │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │
│     background: conic-gradient(                                                                                                      │
│         from 0deg at 50% 50%,                                                                                                        │
│         #ff0080, #ff8c00, #40e0d0,                                                                                                   │
│         #ff0080, #ff8c00, #40e0d0,                                                                                                   │
│         #ff0080                                                                                                                      │
│     );                                                                                                                               │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ## Text Rotation Effects                                                                                                             │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Tilted Heading                                                                                                                   │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ h1 {                                                                                                                                 │
│     transform: rotate(-3deg);                                                                                                        │
│     transform-origin: left center;                                                                                                   │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │

│ ## Fade-In Animations                                                                                                                │

│ ### Simple Fade In                                                                                                                   │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes fadeIn {                                                                                                                  │
│     from { opacity: 0; }                                                                                                             │
│     to { opacity: 1; }                                                                                                               │

│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Fade In Up                                                                                                                       │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes fadeInUp {                                                                                                                │
│     from {                                                                                                                           │
│         opacity: 0;                                                                                                                  │
│         transform: translateY(30px);                                                                                                 │
│     }                                                                                                                                │
│     to {                                                                                                                             │
│         opacity: 1;                                                                                                                  │
│         transform: translateY(0);                                                                                                    │
│     }                                                                                                                                │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │

> I want to generate a Yew/Rust/WASM app that demonstrates an HTML5 layout using sidebars, sections, etc. (the layout-specific HTML5
 elements) with filler content (lorem ipsum), to show different CSS stylings.  There should be a "theme" drop-down that selects
different css files dynamically to change the style.  Start with two themes simple-dark and simple-light that use CSS to support a
dark or light mode.  Start with a new docs dir and new markdown files: architecture, prd, design, plan, and status.  Use Rust for
all domain and presentation logic (minimal JavaScript if any).  Start with vanilla CSS.

⏺ I'll create a Yew/Rust/WASM app that demonstrates HTML5 layout elements with theme switching. Let me start by setting up the
  documentation and then the implementation.

⏺ The project compiles successfully. Here's a summary of what I created:

  Project Structure

  Documentation (docs/):
  - prd.md - Product requirements document
  - architecture.md - System architecture with diagrams
  - design.md - UI/UX design specs and theme colors
  - plan.md - Implementation plan with phases
  - status.md - Current project status (complete)

  Source Code (src/):
  - main.rs - Entry point, initializes Yew renderer
  - app.rs - Root component with theme state and switching logic
  - lorem.rs - Lorem ipsum text constants
  - components/ - All UI components:
    - header.rs - Site header with branding and theme selector
    - theme_selector.rs - Dropdown for theme switching
    - nav.rs - Left sidebar navigation
    - main_content.rs - Main content with sections and articles
    - aside.rs - Right sidebar with links, info, tags
    - footer.rs - Site footer

  Styles (styles/):
  - simple-light.css - Light theme (white background, dark text)
  - simple-dark.css - Dark theme (dark background, light text)

  Both themes use CSS custom properties and CSS Grid for the layout.

  Entry Point:
  - index.html - Trunk-compatible HTML with theme stylesheet link

  To Run

  # Install trunk if needed
  cargo install trunk

  # Start dev server
  trunk serve

  The app demonstrates all HTML5 semantic layout elements (<header>, <nav>, <main>, <section>, <article>, <aside>, <footer>) with a
  dropdown in the header to switch between light and dark themes dynamically—all in pure Rust/WASM with no JavaScript.

       12    pub fn header(props: &HeaderProps) -> Html {
       13 +      let on_change = props.on_theme_change.clone();

       15 +      let onchange = Callback::from(move |e: Event| {

        1 -  mod app;
        1 +  //! Styles POC - A Yew/WASM demo of HTML5 semantic layouts with theme switching.
        2 +

        5

       10 +
       11 +  #[function_component(App)]
       12 +  fn app() -> Html {
       13 +      let theme = use_state(|| "simple-light".to_string());
       14 +
       15 +      let on_theme_change = {
       16 +          let theme = theme.clone();
       17 +          Callback::from(move |new_theme: String| {
       18 +              set_theme(&new_theme);
       19 +              theme.set(new_theme);
       20 +          })
       21 +      };
       22 +
       23 +      html! {
       24 +          <div class="page-layout">
       25 +              <Header theme={(*theme).clone()} on_theme_change={on_theme_change} />
       26 +              <Nav />
       27 +              <MainContent />
       28 +              <Aside />
       29 +              <Footer />
       30 +          </div>
       31 +      }
       32 +  }
       33 +
       34 +  fn set_theme(theme: &str) {
       35 +      let window = web_sys::window().expect("no window");
       36 +      let document = window.document().expect("no document");
       37 +
       38 +      if let Some(link) = document.get_element_by_id("theme-stylesheet") {
       39 +          if let Ok(link) = link.dyn_into::<web_sys::HtmlLinkElement>() {
       40 +              link.set_href(&format!("styles/{theme}.css"));
       41 +          }
       42 +      }
       43 +  }
       44 +
       45    fn main() {
       46        wasm_logger::init(wasm_logger::Config::default());
       47        yew::Renderer::<App>::new().render();

⏺ The skill is set up. Here's a summary of what was created:

  Claude UI Styling Skill Created

  Location

  .claude/skills/ui-styling/
  +-- SKILL.md      # Main skill definition with YAML frontmatter
  +-- patterns.md   # Reference file with CSS patterns and palettes

  How It Works

  1. Automatic Discovery: Claude Code automatically discovers skills in .claude/skills/ directories
  2. Model-Invoked: Unlike slash commands (which you type explicitly), skills are activated by Claude when your request matches the
  skill's description
  3. Description Triggers: The skill description includes trigger terms like:
    - "create new themes"
    - "improve CSS styling"
    - "add visual polish"
    - "make UIs more modern/attractive"
    - "implement design systems"

  How to Use It

  Simply ask Claude to do UI/styling work naturally:

  - "Create a modern minimalist theme"
  - "Add a vibrant, colorful theme to the project"
  - "Make the UI more modern and polished"
  - "Create a dark theme with a purple color scheme"
  - "Improve the CSS styling with better shadows and transitions"

  Claude will automatically use the skill's guidance, including:
  - The project's CSS custom properties convention
  - Color palette suggestions from patterns.md
  - Animation and shadow patterns
  - Accessibility considerations

  To Verify the Skill

  You can ask: "What skills are available?" and Claude should list the ui-styling skill.

  To Add More Themes

  After creating new CSS files with the skill's help, you'll also need to update src/components/header.rs to add the new theme
  option to the dropdown selector.

  Would you like me to commit this skill to the repository, or would you like to test it first by asking me to create a new theme?

│ .claude/skills/wild-ui-styling/patterns.md                                                                                           │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ # Wild CSS Patterns Reference                                                                                                        │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ## Gradient Background Patterns                                                                                                      │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Animated Gradient                                                                                                                │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes gradientShift {                                                                                                           │
│     0% { background-position: 0% 50%; }                                                                                              │
│     50% { background-position: 100% 50%; }                                                                                           │
│     100% { background-position: 0% 50%; }                                                                                            │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │
│     background: linear-gradient(-45deg, #ee7752, #e73c7e, #23a6d5, #23d5ab);                                                         │
│     background-size: 400% 400%;                                                                                                      │
│     animation: gradientShift 15s ease infinite;                                                                                      │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Mesh Gradient                                                                                                                    │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ body {                                                                                                                               │

│ ## Fade-In Animations                                                                                                                │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Simple Fade In                                                                                                                   │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes fadeIn {                                                                                                                  │
│     from { opacity: 0; }                                                                                                             │
│     to { opacity: 1; }                                                                                                               │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ .fade-in {                                                                                                                           │
│     animation: fadeIn 0.8s ease-out forwards;                                                                                        │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Fade In Up                                                                                                                       │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes fadeInUp {                                                                                                                │
│     from {                                                                                                                           │
│         opacity: 0;                                                                                                                  │
│         transform: translateY(30px);                                                                                                 │
│     }                                                                                                                                │
│     to {                                                                                                                             │
│         opacity: 1;                                                                                                                  │
│         transform: translateY(0);                                                                                                    │
│     }                                                                                                                                │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ .fade-in-up {                                                                                                                        │
│     animation: fadeInUp 0.6s ease-out forwards;                                                                                      │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Fade In Scale                                                                                                                    │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ @keyframes fadeInScale {                                                                                                             │
│     from {                                                                                                                           │
│         opacity: 0;                                                                                                                  │
│         transform: scale(0.9);                                                                                                       │
│     }                                                                                                                                │
│     to {                                                                                                                             │
│         opacity: 1;                                                                                                                  │
│         transform: scale(1);                                                                                                         │
│     }                                                                                                                                │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ .fade-in-scale {                                                                                                                     │
│     animation: fadeInScale 0.5s ease-out forwards;                                                                                   │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │
│                                                                                                                                      │
│ ### Staggered Fade In (use with nth-child)                                                                                           │
│ ```css                                                                                                                               │
│ .stagger-item {                                                                                                                      │
│     opacity: 0;                                                                                                                      │
│     animation: fadeInUp 0.5s ease-out forwards;                                                                                      │
│ }                                                                                                                                    │
│ .stagger-item:nth-child(1) { animation-delay: 0.1s; }                                                                                │
│ .stagger-item:nth-child(2) { animation-delay: 0.2s; }                                                                                │
│ .stagger-item:nth-child(3) { animation-delay: 0.3s; }                                                                                │
│ .stagger-item:nth-child(4) { animation-delay: 0.4s; }                                                                                │
│ .stagger-item:nth-child(5) { animation-delay: 0.5s; }                                                                                │
│ ```                                                                                                                                  │

 elements) with filler content (lorem ipsum), to show different CSS stylings.  There should be a "theme" drop-down that selects
different css files dynamically to change the style.  Start with two themes simple-dark and simple-light that use CSS to support a
dark or light mode.  Start with a new docs dir and new markdown files: architecture, prd, design, plan, and status.  Use Rust for
all domain and presentation logic (minimal JavaScript if any).  Start with vanilla CSS.

⏺ The project compiles successfully. Here's a summary of what I created:

  Project Structure

  Documentation (docs/):
  - prd.md - Product requirements document
  - architecture.md - System architecture with diagrams
  - design.md - UI/UX design specs and theme colors
  - plan.md - Implementation plan with phases
  - status.md - Current project status (complete)

  Source Code (src/):
  - main.rs - Entry point, initializes Yew renderer
  - app.rs - Root component with theme state and switching logic
  - lorem.rs - Lorem ipsum text constants
  - components/ - All UI components:
    - header.rs - Site header with branding and theme selector
    - theme_selector.rs - Dropdown for theme switching
    - nav.rs - Left sidebar navigation
    - main_content.rs - Main content with sections and articles
    - aside.rs - Right sidebar with links, info, tags
    - footer.rs - Site footer

  Styles (styles/):
  - simple-light.css - Light theme (white background, dark text)
  - simple-dark.css - Dark theme (dark background, light text)

  Both themes use CSS custom properties and CSS Grid for the layout.

  Entry Point:
  - index.html - Trunk-compatible HTML with theme stylesheet link

  To Run

  # Install trunk if needed
  cargo install trunk

  # Start dev server
  trunk serve

  The app demonstrates all HTML5 semantic layout elements (<header>, <nav>, <main>, <section>, <article>, <aside>, <footer>) with a
  dropdown in the header to switch between light and dark themes dynamically—all in pure Rust/WASM with no JavaScript.

     //! Build script to inject build information at compile time.
     use std::process::Command;
     fn main() {
         // Get hostname
         let hostname = Command::new("hostname")
             .output()
             .map(|o| String::from_utf8_lossy(&o.stdout).trim().to_string())
             .unwrap_or_else(|_| "unknown".to_string());

       11 +  #[function_component(App)]
       12 +  fn app() -> Html {
       13 +      let theme = use_state(|| "simple-light".to_string());
       14 +
       15 +      let on_theme_change = {
       16 +          let theme = theme.clone();
       17 +          Callback::from(move |new_theme: String| {
       18 +              set_theme(&new_theme);
       19 +              theme.set(new_theme);
       20 +          })
       21 +      };
       22 +
       23 +      html! {
       24 +          <div class="page-layout">
       25 +              <Header theme={(*theme).clone()} on_theme_change={on_theme_change} />
       26 +              <Nav />
       27 +              <MainContent />
       28 +              <Aside />
       29 +              <Footer />
       30 +          </div>
       31 +      }
       32 +  }
       33 +
       34 +  fn set_theme(theme: &str) {
       35 +      let window = web_sys::window().expect("no window");
       36 +      let document = window.document().expect("no document");
       37 +
       38 +      if let Some(link) = document.get_element_by_id("theme-stylesheet") {
       39 +          if let Ok(link) = link.dyn_into::<web_sys::HtmlLinkElement>() {
       40 +              link.set_href(&format!("styles/{theme}.css"));
       41 +          }
       42 +      }
       43 +  }
       44 +
       45    fn main() {
       46        wasm_logger::init(wasm_logger::Config::default());
       47        yew::Renderer::<App>::new().render();

⏺ The skill is set up. Here's a summary of what was created:

  Claude UI Styling Skill Created

  Location

  .claude/skills/ui-styling/
  +-- SKILL.md      # Main skill definition with YAML frontmatter
  +-- patterns.md   # Reference file with CSS patterns and palettes

  How It Works

  1. Automatic Discovery: Claude Code automatically discovers skills in .claude/skills/ directories
  2. Model-Invoked: Unlike slash commands (which you type explicitly), skills are activated by Claude when your request matches the
  skill's description
  3. Description Triggers: The skill description includes trigger terms like:
    - "create new themes"
    - "improve CSS styling"
    - "add visual polish"
    - "make UIs more modern/attractive"
    - "implement design systems"

  How to Use It

  Simply ask Claude to do UI/styling work naturally:

  - "Create a modern minimalist theme"
  - "Add a vibrant, colorful theme to the project"
  - "Make the UI more modern and polished"
  - "Create a dark theme with a purple color scheme"
  - "Improve the CSS styling with better shadows and transitions"

  Claude will automatically use the skill's guidance, including:
  - The project's CSS custom properties convention
  - Color palette suggestions from patterns.md
  - Animation and shadow patterns
  - Accessibility considerations

  To Verify the Skill

  You can ask: "What skills are available?" and Claude should list the ui-styling skill.

  To Add More Themes

  After creating new CSS files with the skill's help, you'll also need to update src/components/header.rs to add the new theme
  option to the dropdown selector.

  Would you like me to commit this skill to the repository, or would you like to test it first by asking me to create a new theme?

⏺ Done! I've added the wild-ui-styling skill and two new wild themes:

  wild-ui-styling skill (.claude/skills/wild-ui-styling/):
  - SKILL.md - Guidelines for creating radical, experimental CSS themes
  - patterns.md - Reference patterns for animations, rotations, gradients, neon effects

  wild-light.css - Sunset Fire theme with:
  - Animated gradient background (shifting warm colors)
  - Rotated headings at -2° to -3° angles
  - Staggered fade-in animations for content sections
  - Floating decorative gradient shapes
  - Wiggling header brand animation
  - Navigation links that slide and rotate on hover
  - Tags with bounce/scale hover effects

  wild-dark.css - Neon Cyberpunk theme with:
  - Grid pattern background with continuous animation
  - Scanline overlay effect sweeping across the screen
  - Neon glow shadows (pink, cyan, green)
  - Glitch flicker animations on load
  - Skewed headings with neon pulse
  - Color-cycling animated borders
  - Tags with alternating neon colors
  - Hover effects with neon glow explosions

  The dropdown now has 6 themes: Simple Light, Simple Dark, Improved Light, Improved Dark, Wild Light, and Wild Dark.

  To test: run trunk serve and try switching between themes!
